USER REPORTS (verbatim):
- 故障范围：“所有报文都没有回复”。
- state/error_flags/app_task_iterations：“第一个是1第二个是2，第三个在持续增加”。
- rx/rejected/tx_enqueued：“初始000，然后101到303然后我看了errorflags这次是0”。
  Interpretation in question order: (0,0,0) -> (1,0,1) -> (3,0,3); not a timed loss-rate measurement.
- PCAN Receive after these PINGs：“没有，接收区仍为空”。
- TXBTO/TXBRP：“前者在增加我按了三次变成了7，第二次是3，后者一直为0”。
  TXBTO is a slot bitmask, not a frame count. The observed new completion bit supports controller TX completion, not host application delivery.
- Acceptance Filter：“已确认 Standard，接收范围 000～7FF”。
- Trace screenshot in conversation: one row at 4.9398, CAN-ID321h, Tx, Data, Length8, payload 50 49 4E 47 02 00 00 00; no Rx322 row visible.

- Asked whether BUILD008.03 / 100rpm+500mA result01 / 100rpm+4001mA result02 all match:

  “三项都是 0”。

========== 008.04 DESIGN PREFLIGHT 2026-11-17 ==========
User: 可以了进行下一步
Baseline: Xiao_ODrive / 30aa8d1, commit title 接入 CAN 速度与 Iq 参数.
Starting worktree: only MDK-ARM/FOC_T.uvoptx modified by user; preserved.
Scope of this turn: read-only code/config/HAL review and existing-document design update.
Firmware / IOC / Keil project changes: NONE. New files: NONE. No build/download/hardware operation.

OBSERVED:
- FOC_T.ioc Mcu.IP has no RNG; RCC.RNGFreq_Value=48000000 is only a computed frequency.
- Core/Inc/stm32h7xx_hal_conf.h HAL_RNG_MODULE_ENABLED is commented out.
- No project rng.c/h or HAL RNG driver source/header currently copied.
- main.c SystemClock_Config enables HSI/HSE but not HSI48; main has no MX_RNG_Init.
- Local RCC header supports RNG HSI48; H750 header has HASH_RNG_IRQn=80, RNG CR CED and clock/seed status bits.
- Matching local STM32Cube_FW_H7_V1.12.1 RNG driver available in user Cube repository.
- HAL_RNG_GenerateRandomNumber on H750's no-CONDRST path requires wrapper-level health review, not only HAL_OK.
- Current CPU/HCLK200MHz, FDCAN HSE25MHz, SPI123 HSI64MHz, USART16 PCLK2 100MHz are to remain unchanged.
- Existing command Snapshot is not durable ACK: Publish rejection and STOP can overwrite result/result_seq.
- FDCAN HAL has SOF RxTimestamp and continuous timeout APIs; not enabled by current service.
- HAL tick is TIM7-IRQ driven; pause/long masking requires independent freshness treatment before START.

DECISIONS (PROPOSED, NOT IMPLEMENTED):
- Next small checkpoint 008.04: RNG-generated immutable 128-bit boot identity plus read-only state/four-word query.
- Prefer HSI48 for RNG, startup-only bounded polling, no new DMA/IRQ/task.
- Identity invalid/error prohibits future running sessions; no fallback UID/DWT/fixed identity.
- Random identity is probabilistic, not absolute uniqueness or peer authentication.
- User configures RNG/HSI48 in CubeMX and generates; then review generated diff, integrate code and build both targets.
- Query opcodes05..09 and BUILD008.04 are reserved in spec only; live code remains008.03.
- Complete run transaction, timestamp/timeout final gate and durable consumption result follow independently.

VALIDATION:
Existing-source/configuration review: PASS (limited).
008.04 implementation: NOT_STARTED.
008.04 compile-time checks / BUILD / HOST / HW: NOT_RUN.
Historical008.03 PASS is not reused as proof of new feature.
Only README, ARCHITECTURE version/current-fact correction, spec008 and this evidence file changed.

========== 008.04 IMPLEMENTATION / BUILD / HOST (2026-11-17) ==========
BASE: Xiao_ODrive / 30aa8d1 + generated RNG and this working tree.
HW: NOT_RUN. No download, hardware/power action, stage/commit/push.
Scope: startup-only immutable 128-bit identity and read-only CAN05..09.
No CAN START/UPDATE/session authorization implemented; no output permission change.

GENERATION / INTEGRATION:
- User generated RNG in CubeMX6.16: HSI48 ON, RNG source HSI48 48MHz,
  RNG_CED_ENABLE, HAL_RNG_MODULE_ENABLED, MX_RNG_Init before AppRuntime_Init.
- No RNG IRQ/DMA/new task. Existing PLL, FDCAN500k/SPI/USART/TIM/ADC/RTOS retained.
- CubeMX omitted rng.c/hal_rng.c/hal_rng_ex.c from CommandBench. Added only
  these three entries in existing groups; both targets now64 C +1 assembly.
- Original source/options/macros/Flash-RAM config unchanged. CubeMX set normal
  AfterMake RunUserProg2=1 with empty command name; both actual builds passed.
- Trimmed only redundant project EOF blank line after build; XML semantics same.
- uvoptx user/tool configuration not edited manually; observed SHA256 after
  Bench and Normal builds identical (5DBACAFEFC6DE76EBE7D7DE2A956545274814D72B2097163927DD1AD5E3A7183).

IMPLEMENTATION:
- app_runtime InitBootIdentity once, before scheduler/TIM1 control IRQ.
  Five reads at most: first comparison reference discarded, four candidate words.
  Before/after SR CECS/CEIS/SECS/SEIS and HAL result/state/ErrorCode checked.
  Every zero word and adjacent repetition rejected; no seed recovery/retry.
  All four succeed before private words/state published. Failure sticky.
- Watch mirrors are not authority. Getter copies immutable private state/words,
  zeroes words if invalid; no RNG/heap/IRQ masking in queries.
- CAN request321/reply322 standard/classic/DLC8 unchanged.
  05 state0/1/2;06..09 words. BUILD804, INFO100FF. Invalid provider states
  fail closed; empty provider prevents service initialization.
- Real control/safety files unchanged; 12 relevant control/config files compared
  with HEAD after CRLF normalization, all identical.

BOUNDARIES:
- Hardware RNG quality, boot-to-boot observations, actual chip revision,
  IRQ/timing/stack/electrical behavior NOT verified by HOST.
- HAL timeout is tick difference >2ms and depends on live TIM7 tick; not an
  independent hard deadline. Normal startup requires unsuppressed TIM7.
- MX_RNG_Init/SystemClock failure goes Error_Handler before query service;
  only later wrapper failure can report identity FAILED with CAN alive.
- Random ID is probabilistic, not authentication or complete replay protection.
- RM0433 RNG34.3.5..7/ES0392 Rev16 reviewed; zero-word check per RM,
  adjacent-repeat/reference-word policy is additional conservative software check.
- No claim that old HW PASS covers new AXF.

VALIDATION:

  covers each read failure, sticky/current errors, zero/repeat, no partial
  publication, no retry, private state vs Watch, repeated read/reset models.
  New single tests/run_runtime_boot_host.py; generated harness ignored in build/.

IMPLEMENTED:
Providers copied, 9 frames pages30..38, explicit39 result query; BUILD807/INFO103FF;
complete128bit boot identity+epoch+request ID+target pair+operation+originalSOF TTL.
CRC32 IEEE reflected EDB88320/init+finalFFFFFFFF (error detection, not auth).
Golden words [1,1,2,3,4,1,01F40064,03E80001] LE32 -> 80B3BFF2.
One in-progress64B slot plus one64B completed cache; highwater prevents reuse.
Duplicate same content rereads, no resubmit/renew; malformed/old/mixed data denied.
Core receipt cached before next normal Submit; internal/externalseq are distinct.
INTERNAL result means outcome may be uncertain; private time is fenced LOST.
Correct first COMMIT after precommit cancellation may read its existing outcome,
but cannot reopen/submit; wrong CRC returns conflict without altering cache.

Original16bit SOF bound to pending/active12B sidecars in same PRIMASK publication.
HAL frame window is auxiliary; original hardware deadline checked at Submit,
BeginCycle and FinalPermit. task ObserveTime before every CanLink_Poll latches
expired internal sequences permanently, covering TIM1 stalled with task running.
Task stall covered by stickyTOO; hardware clock stopped/debugger tampering excluded.
Task does not revoke Core/output; TIM1 disarms before Revoke, oldFAULT/STOP precedence.
Original Frame32/Core/Snapshot72/Receipt12 layouts unchanged.
W1C loss/fatal flags now cleared only AFTER private time LOST published, fixing
a new IRQ-observer race. Direct lease guard has no HAL calls/waits/reloads.

SRC: independent cross-module review, expiry/wrap/W1C issues fixed and tested.
HOST: real production code with CPU/HAL/register/time stubs, not hardware emulation.

  FastISR Begin 1734 -> Final 18a0 -> output 18ce -> DMB 1904 -> CaptureReadyLoss 194c.

  Arm DMB 053c -> state 0548; Complete two interval stores 05f6/05fc -> DMB 0614 -> state 0620.

pending 时在解屏蔽以后处理配对。无待处理记录时发布路径仍增加状态判断开销；
